# Linux — inferred

The **7,307** projects that probably run on Linux but do not say so. Each verdict here comes from the install route or the language runtime, never from a release asset or from the README — that is what puts them on this page rather than with the [2,746 confirmed ones](linux.md).

Read `Why` before trusting a row. A portable runtime is a good bet and not a promise.

[← Linux](linux.md) · [← back to the mega list](../README.md)

Page **8** of 10, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. In order: [1](linux-inferred.md) · [2](linux-inferred-2.md) · [3](linux-inferred-3.md) · [4](linux-inferred-4.md) · [5](linux-inferred-5.md) · [6](linux-inferred-6.md) · [7](linux-inferred-7.md) · **8** · [9](linux-inferred-9.md) · [10](linux-inferred-10.md) — [continue on page 9 →](linux-inferred-9.md)
